# 1 · Craft list (coverage list)

> **Phase 1 result (2026-11-19, LO reviewed every drop in chat).** 2,173 items: **port 1,600** (1,596 tagged by the readers, plus 4 that LO made rules: C2024, C2083, C2167, C2168) · **merge 446** · **drop 74** · **idea 18** (go to `LATER.md` / `STATE.md` / the piece card, per the LO column) · **moved 35** (31 to `2_checks.md`, 4 to `3_lessons.md` as L162–L165). Port and merge rows were not reviewed one by one; they stand as tagged until Phase 3 ports them.

Harvested 2026-11-19 from `.claude/skills/author-game-v2/` (frozen at `ea8f6465`). Sources are relative to that folder. Read-only harvest: six readers each read every line of their files; the lead merged and numbered. **Ids never renumber.**

- Files: `SKILL.md`, all 23 `references/*.md`, all 18 `templates/cards/*.md`.
- Tags: *port* = real craft · *merge* = same as the item named · *drop* = process only (reason given).
- Evidence: *measured* (study or field number) or *stated*.
- The **LO** column records LO's answer, dated, in his words. Every item must end *ported* or *dropped by LO*.
- Many *merge* rows from `SKILL.md`'s gate table (the P-reader's) point at the reference that argues the rule; the check itself is in `2_checks.md`.
- v2.py line numbers cited inside ideas will drift; re-grep before trusting one.

## Reader notes: conflicts and superseded text found while reading


### SKILL.md + process files

- Contradiction: C424 (meek/bratty/neutral variant on every player line) against C1344 (the game had no personality axis, so 33 such lines shipped as random rotation). Port only together: the variant needs a real meter, or it's openly random.
- C2014, C2013, C2056, C2073 (sheets as the review surface; "a sandbox can't be reviewed by playing") are superseded by PLAN's goal and by SKILL.md:692-694 itself (LO's first ten minutes found more than every gate).
- C753/C754 (minimum viable mass: 3,000-word median per location before v0.1 ships) conflicts with PLAN "release 1 is a skeleton playable in one sitting". LO needs to decide. C2088 (cadence numbers are for mature games only) bears on it too.
- Door doctrine changed: SKILL.md:269 gate and state.md:485 still talk about a door, but the-release.md:208-214 says a locked door is no longer required. C86 is merged into C140.
- C736, P353, C236, C1338, C1243, C131 and C135 are placeholder rows kept so cross-references resolve. The lead may delete them on renumbering.
- Most SKILL.md gate rows (C712-C726) are merges into other files. The lead should check that those files' harvests carry the idea. Ideas that exist only here are tagged port: C1700 reach check, C1721 can-happen check, C1709 artefact check, C1644 save diff.
- C219 / SKILL.md:43 says no per-beat field figure exists, so the 7.5% floor is a "not empty" check only.

### register, voice, heat, moments

- Frozen word list itself is not in these files; it lives in `gates.py` (only referenced). Measured explicit numbers: 3+ per beat (C254), 7.5% floor (C255, DoL 6.8-11.3%), field median 33.3% (C256), 50% explicit beats with own clip (C1253). Model beats carry scored numbers (C481-C150).
- Superseded doctrine still present: heat-ladder.md:49 says the old rule "a group, known or not, is stage 4" is replaced (by C291); register.md:1324 notes v1 Rule 10 moved to the-clock.md.
- Possible tension: C475/C457 (field median sentence 10, gate ceiling 14) vs model beats running median 4-9 "on purpose"; register.md says the voice wins over measured rules (C440).
- Possible tension: C259 "rewrite in place, not additive" vs C477 "restore contrast" (adds words); C478 resolves it (contrast, not volume).
- C268 (register test 10) and C311 (H4) state the same low-heat contents; heat-ladder is the fuller owner.
- the-voice.md R3 vs R3b looked contradictory (no number vs print the number); C1359 records the reconciliation (engine prints the count, words never carry it).
- The 50 moment-library entries are evidence quotes; only the five kinds and their stated reasons are listed as ideas (C153-C154).

### want, arc, first hour, returning player

- Superseded doctrine still present: the-arc.md:203-209 keeps family-ties' "raise stops at the next threshold" as evidence but marks it "not this skill's rule since 2026-11-09"; captured as C333 merged into C330 (pay continues across the line).
- the-arc.md:206 says stopping at her own threshold made the playtest wall, while the-arc.md:186 caps at "the stage above"; consistent, but the older the-arc.md A4 text and A4d say it twice (C330/C339).
- the-want.md:225 and :353 (design what stops her) are scoped by :356 to people outside the house only; C319 vs C320/C321 must travel together or the port contradicts the heat ladder.
- the-first-hour.md F2b's screen-walk table (C2134) is sheet ceremony, but its engine facts (age gate is screen 0, CustomizeCharacters text is hard-coded, one node = one screen) are real and kept as C1780-C522.
- the-first-hour.md:684 says requires_npc is "now" read on the solo lane (corrected 9-03) while :611-626 lists the same; no conflict, just duplicated (C1792/C1793/C1796).
- the-first-hour.md TOC lists "11" twice and has no F2b/F5b entries; doc defect only.
- Many engine facts cite v2.py line numbers that will drift; the porter should re-grep before trusting any line number (e.g. v2.py:5942 is cited both for renderSoloActivities and for the portrait selector skip).
- the-want.md:194 and :210 call the bill "first by position in this file"; the order of holds in the port should follow the measured frequency (ambition first).

### surfaces, map, clock, media-blocks

- Tension: S:878 quotes v1 lanes.md "the hub opener is ONE constant paragraph, do not tier" while S:883 (LO D56 a) lets level bands rewrite everything under the identity line. C799 is the newer call; C798's "do not tier the opener" should be read as "do not tier the identity line".
- S:1160 "video on outcome nodes, images on hubs" is coarser than media-blocks' by-kind table (C1262-C1265); the table should win.
- C:340-345 says the proposed `locked_text` gate was killed, but S:680-711 and S:1188 say gate 42 "a locked door says why" exists. One of them is stale.
- The clock cheat sheet cites line numbers that differ from the body: travel tag v2.py:6077 (C:365) vs 6237 (C:203); show_when_blocked v2.py:13572 (C:368) vs 15095/14549. It also says "C2 carries the split" (C:362) when it is C3.
- P:379 (the map is signed like a spine page) is superseded by PLAN's goal that LO never signs sheets.
- S:740-745 (pass-list games, thresholds dominate, sexual meter gates 3-15%) undercuts the 53/31 field split at S:727; port both, but the pass-list number is the one to use for our games.
- S:243 and S:878 cite the v1 author-game skill (lanes.md) as evidence; the evidence should be restated without v1 if v1 is going away.
- S:220-239 and S:1213-1219 are dated "COMPLETED" and "half-true" update boxes around older text, which is doc self-maintenance. The ideas are kept, the boxes are dropped.

### meters, economy, systems, phone, cards

- The economy file R3 (a recurring bill in 14 of 19 games) was measured on a male-lead corpus. The file corrects this itself at the-economy.md:241 (female-lead field 6 of 23). Port R3 only as the mechanism for a game that picks the bill (C72), not as a default.
- the-meters.md W2 vs the-heat-ladder H1: a stage cap on an act (M6, A4d) is allowed, but W4b now says acts pay *across* the line. The two fit (cap at the top of the stage above). Older text that caps an act at its own stage top is superseded by C1400.
- the-systems.md SY2's one-source rule has an exception for ascent tiers (C1451, LO F31). Without it, SY2 contradicts the-heat-ladder H5. Keep the two together.
- the-phone.md P3 says "7 words or fewer", but the phone study's own bubble-marked games ran a median of 11–16 words (the-phone.md:117). The 7 is LO's call against part of the evidence.
- the-phone.md P5/P9 require ignore costs and a no-show cost. P11 bans phone upkeep. Both stand: a cost trades against something, upkeep does not.
- SY6 "the notice" and pregnancy's portrait-without-wardrobe fix are engine wishlist items, not built (C1903, C1904, C1872). They are tagged port as engine facts. They should not become authoring rules.
- Card "Our engine today" rows repeat stat-based value (`setup.resolveEffectValue`) and "computed bill not built" many times. These are folded into C1890, C1920 and C1925.
- Card evidence (round 9a/9b traces) is cited by passage name, not file:line, so I marked it "measured" on the trace's say-so.
- Some "port" rows in the cards are rich model facts (River Rat's 15 rungs, CoT pregnancy days). The new skill may want them as one worked example per card rather than as rules.

### engine.md

- Superseded doctrine still present in the file: overnight two-row note (C922 vs C921); old "omitted clamp = true" (C1974 vs C1973); random-cooldown "unverified" (C1982 vs C1583, 3 arrivals); block_pool nesting "unverified, don't nest" at :2232 vs importer silently drops it at :2161 (C1993); "rejection_node typo is silent" at :2310 vs "build-validated" at :2318 (C1995); one-terminal_text cap scoped away (C2169); rent grace/eviction route vs "no game over over rent" (C1064).
- §1 (`is_repeatable` default TRUE) and §31/§42 interplay: "non-repeatable renders no portrait" is stated twice (:1737 and :2776), merged into C689.
- §19 advice ("make the second canvas a triggerless rung") is explicitly scoped; a new skill must keep the scope or it re-teaches "one paragraph and a wall of buttons".
- Line :71 says omitted clamp emitted false on choice/node/entry effects — consistent with §21; only the old §21 text was wrong.
- Several ideas (C2172, C2173, C1072 scoreboard) depend on gates.py surviving; tagged drop/port accordingly.
- Area choice: saves facts filed under "returning player" (the file defers authoring rules to the-returning-player.md); pure TOML/key traps under "engine".
